        const pieceCodes = {
          wk: "wk.png",
          wq: "wq.png",
          wr: "wr.png",
          wb: "wb.png",
          wn: "wn.png",
          wp: "wp.png",
          bk: "bk.png",
          bq: "bq.png",
          br: "br.png",
          bb: "bb.png",
          bn: "bn.png",
          bp: "bp.png",
        };
        const pieceImages = {};

        function getCurrentPieceSet() {
          try {
            const s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("chesshub_settings"));
            return s?.pieceSet || "neo";
          } catch {
            return "neo";
          }
        }

        function loadPieces() {
          return new Promise((resolve) => {
            const pieceSet = getCurrentPieceSet();
            let loaded = 0;
            const total = Object.keys(pieceCodes).length;
            for (const [key, filename] of Object.entries(pieceCodes)) {
              const img = new Image();
              img.onload = img.onerror = () => {
                loaded++;
                if (loaded === total) resolve();
              };
              img.src = `pieces/${pieceSet}/${filename}`;
              pieceImages[key] = img;
            }
          });
        }

        document.addEventListener("pieceSetChanged", function () {
          loadPieces();
        });

        function getBoardColors() {
          const root = document.documentElement;
          const light =
            getComputedStyle(root).getPropertyValue("--board-light").trim() ||
            "#f0d9b5";
          const dark =
            getComputedStyle(root).getPropertyValue("--board-dark").trim() ||
            "#b58863";
          return { light, dark };
        }

        let currentPlayerGames = [];
        let filteredGames = [];
        let currentGameMoves = [];
        let currentMoveIndex = 0;
        let currentGame = null;
        let autoPlayInterval = null;
        let isAutoPlaying = false;
        let totalPlies = 0;

        const playersGrid = document.getElementById("playersGrid");
        const gamesOverlay = document.getElementById("gamesOverlay");
        const gameOverlay = document.getElementById("gameOverlay");
        const gamesListContainer =
          document.getElementById("gamesListContainer");
        const gameViewerContainer = document.getElementById(
          "gameViewerContainer",
        );
        const overlayPlayerName = document.getElementById("overlayPlayerName");
        const closeGamesOverlayBtn =
          document.getElementById("closeGamesOverlay");
        const closeGameOverlayBtn = document.getElementById("closeGameOverlay");
        const searchInput = document.getElementById("searchInput");
        const searchField = document.getElementById("searchField");
        const resetSearchBtn = document.getElementById("resetSearchBtn");
        const playerCount = document.getElementById("playerCount");

        function renderPlayers() {
          if (!playersGrid) return;
          playersGrid.innerHTML = "";
          players.forEach((p) => {
            const card = document.createElement("div");
            card.className = "player-card";
            card.innerHTML = `
              <div class="avatar">
                <img src="${p.avatar}" alt="${p.name}" onerror="this.src='https://ui-avatars.com/api/?background=2c3e50&color=fff&name=${encodeURIComponent(p.name)}'" loading="lazy">
              </div>
              <div class="name">${p.name}</div>
              <div class="title">${p.title}</div>
            `;
            card.addEventListener("click", function () {
              openPlayerGames(p);
            });
            playersGrid.appendChild(card);
          });
          if (playerCount) playerCount.textContent = `${players.length} بازیکن`;
        }

        function openPlayerGames(player) {
          if (!player) return;
          if (overlayPlayerName) overlayPlayerName.textContent = player.name;
          if (gamesOverlay) gamesOverlay.classList.add("open");
          document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";

          if (searchInput) searchInput.value = "";
          if (gameOverlay) gameOverlay.classList.remove("open");
          if (gameViewerContainer) gameViewerContainer.innerHTML = "";

          if (gamesListContainer) {
            gamesListContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="loading-state"><i class="fas fa-spinner fa-pulse"></i> بارگذاری بازی‌های ${player.name}...</div>`;
          }

          fetch(`pgn/${player.file}`)
            .then((res) => {
              if (!res.ok) throw new Error("فایل یافت نشد");
              return res.text();
            })
            .then((pgnText) => {
              currentPlayerGames = parsePGN(pgnText);
              filteredGames = [...currentPlayerGames];
              renderGamesList(filteredGames);
            })
            .catch((err) => {
              console.error(err);
              if (gamesListContainer) {
                gamesListContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="no-games">❌ خطا در بارگذاری بازی‌ها</div>`;
              }
            });
        }

        function parsePGN(pgnText) {
          const rawGames = pgnText.split(/(?=\[Event ")/);
          const games = [];
          for (let raw of rawGames) {
            if (!raw.trim()) continue;
            const whiteMatch = raw.match(/\[White "([^"]+)"\]/);
            const blackMatch = raw.match(/\[Black "([^"]+)"\]/);
            const resultMatch = raw.match(/\[Result "([^"]+)"\]/);
            const dateMatch = raw.match(/\[Date "([^"]+)"\]/);
            const ecoMatch = raw.match(/\[ECO "([^"]+)"\]/);
            const openingMatch = raw.match(/\[Opening "([^"]+)"\]/);
            games.push({
              white: whiteMatch ? whiteMatch[1] : "?",
              black: blackMatch ? blackMatch[1] : "?",
              result: resultMatch ? resultMatch[1] : "*",
              date: dateMatch ? dateMatch[1] : "????.??.??",
              eco: ecoMatch ? ecoMatch[1] : "",
              opening: openingMatch ? openingMatch[1] : "",
              pgn: raw,
            });
          }
          return games;
        }

        function renderGamesList(games) {
          if (!gamesListContainer) return;
          if (!games.length) {
            gamesListContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="no-games">هیچ بازی‌ای با این جستجو یافت نشد</div>`;
            return;
          }
          gamesListContainer.innerHTML = "";
          games.forEach((game, idx) => {
            const div = document.createElement("div");
            div.className = "game-item";
            const resultText =
              game.result === "1-0"
                ? "برد سفید"
                : game.result === "0-1"
                  ? "برد سیاه"
                  : "مساوی";
            const resultClass =
              game.result === "1-0"
                ? "white-win"
                : game.result === "0-1"
                  ? "black-win"
                  : "draw";
            div.innerHTML = `
              <div class="game-info">
                <span class="badge ${resultClass}">${resultText}</span>
                <span>⚪ ${game.white}</span>
                <span>⚫ ${game.black}</span>
                <span>📅 ${game.date}</span>
                ${game.opening ? `<span class="opening-tag">${game.opening}</span>` : ""}
              </div>
              <div class="game-arrow"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></div>
            `;
            div.addEventListener("click", function () {
              openGameViewer(idx, games);
            });
            gamesListContainer.appendChild(div);
          });
        }

        function performSearch() {
          if (!searchInput || !searchField) return;
          const query = searchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();
          const field = searchField.value;
          if (!query) {
            filteredGames = [...currentPlayerGames];
            renderGamesList(filteredGames);
            return;
          }
          filteredGames = currentPlayerGames.filter((game) => {
            if (field === "all") {
              return (
                game.white.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
                game.black.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
                game.result.includes(query) ||
                game.date.includes(query) ||
                (game.opening && game.opening.toLowerCase().includes(query))
              );
            }
            if (field === "white") {
              return game.white.toLowerCase().includes(query);
            }
            if (field === "black") {
              return game.black.toLowerCase().includes(query);
            }
            if (field === "result") {
              const map = {
                "1-0": "برد سفید",
                "0-1": "برد سیاه",
                "*": "مساوی",
              };
              return (
                map[game.result]?.includes(query) || game.result.includes(query)
              );
            }
            if (field === "date") {
              return game.date.includes(query);
            }
            return false;
          });
          renderGamesList(filteredGames);
        }

        function openGameViewer(gameIdx, games) {
          if (!games || !games[gameIdx]) return;
          const game = games[gameIdx];
          currentGame = game;
          stopAutoPlay();
          if (!gameViewerContainer) return;

          const viewer = document.createElement("div");
          viewer.className = "game-viewer";
          viewer.id = "gameViewer";
          const resultIcon =
            game.result === "1-0"
              ? "🏆 برد سفید"
              : game.result === "0-1"
                ? "🏆 برد سیاه"
                : "🤝 مساوی";

          viewer.innerHTML = `
            <div class="viewer-header">
              <div class="game-title">♜ ${game.white} <span class="vs">vs</span> ${game.black} <span style="font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-light);font-weight:400;margin-right:0.4rem;">${resultIcon}</span></div>
            </div>
            <div class="viewer-body">
              <div class="board-wrapper">
                <div id="gameBoard" class="chessboard"></div>
                <div class="playback-controls">
                  <button id="firstMoveBtn"><i class="fas fa-fast-backward"></i></button>
                  <button id="prevMoveBtn"><i class="fas fa-step-backward"></i></button>
                  <button id="playPauseBtn" class="primary"><i class="fas fa-play"></i> شروع</button>
                  <button id="nextMoveBtn"><i class="fas fa-step-forward"></i></button>
                  <button id="lastMoveBtn"><i class="fas fa-fast-forward"></i></button>
                  <span class="move-counter" id="moveCounter">0/0</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="info-wrapper">
                <div class="game-meta">
                  <div class="meta-item"><span class="label">گشایش:</span><span class="value">${game.opening || "نامشخص"}</span></div>
                  <div class="meta-item"><span class="label">ECO:</span><span class="value">${game.eco || "-"}</span></div>
                  <div class="meta-item"><span class="label">تاریخ:</span><span class="value">${game.date}</span></div>
                  <div class="meta-item"><span class="label">تعداد حرکت:</span><span class="value" id="totalMoves">0</span></div>
                </div>
                <div class="pgn-actions">
                  <button id="copyPgnBtn"><i class="fas fa-copy"></i> کپی PGN</button>
                  <button id="downloadPgnBtn"><i class="fas fa-download"></i> دانلود PGN</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;

          gameViewerContainer.innerHTML = "";
          gameViewerContainer.appendChild(viewer);
          loadGameMoves(game.pgn);

          document
            .getElementById("firstMoveBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", goToFirstMove);
          document
            .getElementById("prevMoveBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", goToPrevMove);
          document
            .getElementById("playPauseBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", toggleAutoPlay);
          document
            .getElementById("nextMoveBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", goToNextMove);
          document
            .getElementById("lastMoveBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", goToLastMove);
          document
            .getElementById("copyPgnBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", copyPGN);
          document
            .getElementById("downloadPgnBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", downloadPGN);

          if (gameOverlay) gameOverlay.classList.add("open");
          document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
          if (gameOverlay) gameOverlay.scrollTop = 0;
        }

        function loadGameMoves(pgn) {
          const temp = new Chess();
          try {
            temp.load_pgn(pgn);
            currentGameMoves = temp.history({ verbose: true });
          } catch (e) {
            currentGameMoves = [];
          }
          totalPlies = currentGameMoves.length;
          currentMoveIndex = 0;
          const moveCount = Math.ceil(totalPlies / 2);
          const totalMovesEl = document.getElementById("totalMoves");
          if (totalMovesEl) totalMovesEl.textContent = moveCount;
          updateBoard();
        }

        function renderBoardDOM(boardId, moves, moveIndex) {
          const container = document.getElementById(boardId);
          if (!container) return;
          const game = new Chess();
          for (let i = 0; i < moveIndex; i++) {
            game.move(moves[i]);
          }
          const board = game.board();
          const pieceSet = getCurrentPieceSet();
          container.innerHTML = "";
          container.style.display = "grid";
          container.style.gridTemplateColumns = "repeat(8, 1fr)";
          container.style.aspectRatio = "1 / 1";
          for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
              const piece = board[i][j];
              const isLight = (i + j) % 2 === 0;
              const squareDiv = document.createElement("div");
              squareDiv.className = `square ${isLight ? "light" : "dark"}`;
              if (piece) {
                const key =
                  (piece.color === "w" ? "w" : "b") + piece.type.toLowerCase();
                const img = document.createElement("img");
                img.src = `pieces/${pieceSet}/${pieceCodes[key]}`;
                img.classList.add("piece-img");
                img.draggable = false;
                img.style.pointerEvents = "none";
                squareDiv.appendChild(img);
              }
              container.appendChild(squareDiv);
            }
          }
        }

        function updateBoard() {
          renderBoardDOM("gameBoard", currentGameMoves, currentMoveIndex);
          const moveNumber = Math.ceil(currentMoveIndex / 2);
          const totalMoves = Math.ceil(totalPlies / 2);
          const counter = document.getElementById("moveCounter");
          if (counter) counter.textContent = `${moveNumber}/${totalMoves}`;
        }

        function goToFirstMove() {
          stopAutoPlay();
          currentMoveIndex = 0;
          updateBoard();
        }
        function goToPrevMove() {
          stopAutoPlay();
          if (currentMoveIndex > 0) currentMoveIndex--;
          updateBoard();
        }
        function goToNextMove() {
          stopAutoPlay();
          if (currentMoveIndex < totalPlies) currentMoveIndex++;
          updateBoard();
        }
        function goToLastMove() {
          stopAutoPlay();
          currentMoveIndex = totalPlies;
          updateBoard();
        }

        function toggleAutoPlay() {
          if (isAutoPlaying) stopAutoPlay();
          else startAutoPlay();
        }
        function startAutoPlay() {
          if (isAutoPlaying) return;
          if (currentMoveIndex >= totalPlies) {
            currentMoveIndex = 0;
            updateBoard();
          }
          isAutoPlaying = true;
          const btn = document.getElementById("playPauseBtn");
          if (btn) btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-pause"></i> توقف';
          autoPlayInterval = setInterval(() => {
            if (currentMoveIndex < totalPlies) {
              currentMoveIndex++;
              updateBoard();
            } else {
              stopAutoPlay();
            }
          }, 1200);
        }
        function stopAutoPlay() {
          isAutoPlaying = false;
          if (autoPlayInterval) {
            clearInterval(autoPlayInterval);
            autoPlayInterval = null;
          }
          const btn = document.getElementById("playPauseBtn");
          if (btn) btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-play"></i> شروع';
        }

        function copyPGN() {
          if (!currentGame) return;
          navigator.clipboard
            .writeText(currentGame.pgn)
            .then(() => alert("✅ PGN کپی شد"))
            .catch(() => alert("❌ خطا در کپی PGN"));
        }
        function downloadPGN() {
          if (!currentGame) return;
          const blob = new Blob([currentGame.pgn], { type: "text/plain" });
          const a = document.createElement("a");
          const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          a.href = url;
          a.download = `${currentGame.white}_vs_${currentGame.black}.pgn`;
          document.body.appendChild(a);
          a.click();
          document.body.removeChild(a);
          URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        }

        function closeGameOverlay() {
          if (gameOverlay) gameOverlay.classList.remove("open");
          if (!gamesOverlay || !gamesOverlay.classList.contains("open")) {
            document.body.style.overflow = "";
          }
          stopAutoPlay();
          if (gameViewerContainer) gameViewerContainer.innerHTML = "";
        }
        function closeGamesOverlay() {
          if (gamesOverlay) gamesOverlay.classList.remove("open");
          if (gameOverlay) gameOverlay.classList.remove("open");
          document.body.style.overflow = "";
          stopAutoPlay();
          if (gameViewerContainer) gameViewerContainer.innerHTML = "";
        }

        function init() {
          loadPieces().then(() => {
            renderPlayers();
            if (closeGamesOverlayBtn)
              closeGamesOverlayBtn.addEventListener("click", closeGamesOverlay);
            if (closeGameOverlayBtn)
              closeGameOverlayBtn.addEventListener("click", closeGameOverlay);
            if (searchInput)
              searchInput.addEventListener("input", performSearch);
            if (searchField)
              searchField.addEventListener("change", performSearch);
            if (resetSearchBtn)
              resetSearchBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (searchInput) searchInput.value = "";
                performSearch();
              });

            document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
              if (e.key === "Escape") {
                if (gameOverlay && gameOverlay.classList.contains("open"))
                  closeGameOverlay();
                else if (
                  gamesOverlay &&
                  gamesOverlay.classList.contains("open")
                )
                  closeGamesOverlay();
              }
            });

            document.addEventListener("pieceSetChanged", function () {
              loadPieces().then(() => {
                if (currentGame) updateBoard();
              });
            });
            document.addEventListener("themeChanged", function () {
              if (currentGame) updateBoard();
            });
          });
        }

        if (document.readyState === "loading") {
          document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", init);
        } else {
          init();
        }
      })();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

    </div>

    <script src="js/script.js"></script>
    <script src="js/puzzle.js"></script>
    <script>
      (function applySavedTheme() {
        try {
          const settings = JSON.parse(
            localStorage.getItem("chesshub_settings"),
          );
          if (settings && settings.theme) {
            document.body.classList.add(`theme-${settings.theme}`);
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      })();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

FILE: C:\Users\VICTUS\chesshub\online.html

<!doctype html>
<html lang="fa" dir="rtl">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <meta
      name="viewport"
      content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no, viewport-fit=cover"
    />
    <title>ChessHub | بازی آنلاین</title>
    <link
      rel="stylesheet"
      href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0-beta3/css/all.min.css"
    />
    <link
      href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/rastikerdar/vazir-font@v30.1.0/dist/font-face.css"
      rel="stylesheet"
    />
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="css/style.css" />
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/chess.js/0.10.3/chess.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.socket.io/4.4.1/socket.io.min.js"></script>
    <style>
      .online-wrapper {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        margin-bottom: 2rem;
      }
      .game-card {
        background: #fff;
        border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
        padding: 1.5rem;
        box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 600px;
      }
      .status-bar {
        background: var(--primary-dark);
        color: #fff;
        padding: 1rem;
        border-radius: var(--radius-md);
        text-align: center;
        font-weight: 800;
        margin-bottom: 1rem;
        font-size: 1.1rem;
      }
      .status-bar.waiting {
        background: var(--warning);
        color: #000;
      }
      #chessboard {
        width: 100%;
        aspect-ratio: 1/1;
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(8, 1fr);
        border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
        overflow: hidden;
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-dark);
      }
      .square {
        aspect-ratio: 1/1;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        cursor: pointer;
        position: relative;
      }
      .square.light {
        background-color: #f0d9b5;
      }
      .square.dark {
        background-color: #b58863;
      }
      .square.selected {
        background-color: #f6e05e !important;
      }
      .piece-img {
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        pointer-events: none;
        user-select: none;
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        z-index: 1;
      }
      .message-box {
        margin-top: 1rem;
        padding: 1rem;
        background: #f8fafc;
        border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
        border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
        text-align: center;
        font-weight: 700;
      }
    </style>
  </head>
  <body>
    <div class="container">
      <div id="header-placeholder"></div>
      <section class="app-hero">
        <h1><i class="fas fa-globe"></i> بازی آنلاین</h1>
        <p>سیستم ضد تقلب و پایدار با توکن اختصاصی!</p>
      </section>

      <div class="online-wrapper">
        <div class="game-card">
          <div class="status-bar waiting" id="statusBar">
            در حال اتصال به سرور...
          </div>
          <div id="chessboard"></div>
          <div class="message-box" id="msgBox">لطفاً صبر کنید.</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="footer-placeholder"></div>
    </div>

    <script src="js/script.js"></script>
    <script>
      (function () {
        "use strict";

        // آدرس سرور (لوکال یا رندر)
        const socket = io("http://localhost:3000");
        let game = new Chess();
        let boardSquares = {};
        let selectedSquare = null;
        let myColor = "w";
        let isFlipped = false;
        let currentGameId = localStorage.getItem("chesshub_game_id");
        let myName = localStorage.getItem("chesshub_player_name") || "Player";
        const pieceCodes = {
          wk: "wk.png",
          wq: "wq.png",
          wr: "wr.png",
          wb: "wb.png",
          wn: "wn.png",
          wp: "wp.png",
          bk: "bk.png",
          bq: "bq.png",
          br: "br.png",
          bb: "bb.png",
          bn: "bn.png",
          bp: "bp.png",
        };
        const boardDiv = document.getElementById("chessboard");

        function buildBoard() {
          boardDiv.innerHTML = "";
          boardSquares = {};
          for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
              const row = isFlipped ? 7 - i : i,
                col = isFlipped ? 7 - j : j;
              const sqName = String.fromCharCode(97 + col) + (8 - row);
              const sq = document.createElement("div");
              sq.className = `square ${(row + col) % 2 === 0 ? "light" : "dark"}`;
              sq.dataset.square = sqName;
              boardDiv.appendChild(sq);
              boardSquares[sqName] = sq;
            }
          }
          updateBoard();
        }

        function updateBoard() {
          const b = game.board();
          for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
              const row = isFlipped ? 7 - i : i,
                col = isFlipped ? 7 - j : j;
              const p = b[row][col];
              const sqName = String.fromCharCode(97 + col) + (8 - row);
              const sqData = boardSquares[sqName];
              sqData.innerHTML = "";
              sqData.classList.remove("selected");
              if (p) {
                const key =
                  (p.color === "w" ? "w" : "b") + p.type.toLowerCase();
                const img = document.createElement("img");
                img.src = `pieces/neo/${pieceCodes[key]}`;
                img.classList.add("piece-img");
                sqData.appendChild(img);
              }
            }
          }
          if (selectedSquare)
            boardSquares[selectedSquare].classList.add("selected");
        }

        function makeMove(from, to, promo = "q") {
          socket.emit("makeMove", {
            gameId: currentGameId,
            from,
            to,
            promotion: promo,
          });
        }

        // --- منطق درگ‌اند‌دراپ ---
        let dragStart = null,
          isDragging = false,
          dragClone = null,
          rafId = null,
          lastX = 0,
          lastY = 0,
          cloneSize = 0;
        function createDragClone(sq, cx, cy) {
          const d = boardSquares[sq];
          if (!d) return null;
          const img = d.querySelector("img");
          if (!img) return null;
          const r = d.getBoundingClientRect();
          cloneSize = r.width;
          const c = img.cloneNode(true);
          c.style.cssText = `position:fixed;pointer-events:none;z-index:9999;width:${cloneSize}px;height:${cloneSize}px;left:0;top:0;transform:translate3d(${cx - cloneSize / 2}px,${cy - cloneSize / 2}px,0) scale(1.05);`;
          document.body.appendChild(c);
          img.style.opacity = "0";
          return c;
        }
        function updDrag() {
          if (dragClone)
            dragClone.style.transform = `translate3d(${lastX - cloneSize / 2}px,${lastY - cloneSize / 2}px,0) scale(1.05)`;
          rafId = null;
        }
        function onDown(e) {
          if (game.turn() !== myColor || game.game_over()) return;
          const cx = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX,
            cy = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
          const el = document.elementFromPoint(cx, cy);
          const sq = el?.closest?.(".square");
          if (!sq) return;
          const s = sq.dataset.square;
          const p = game.get(s);
          if (p && p.color === myColor) {
            e.preventDefault();
            dragStart = s;
            isDragging = true;
            dragClone = createDragClone(s, cx, cy);
          } else if (selectedSquare) {
            makeMove(selectedSquare, s);
            selectedSquare = null;
            updateBoard();
          }
        }
        function onMove(e) {
          if (!isDragging) return;
          e.preventDefault();
          lastX = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
          lastY = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
          if (!rafId) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(updDrag);
        }
        function onUp(e) {
          if (!isDragging || !dragStart) {
            cleanup();
            return;
          }
          const cx = e.changedTouches ? e.changedTouches[0].clientX : e.clientX,
            cy = e.changedTouches ? e.changedTouches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
          if (dragClone) {
            dragClone.remove();
            dragClone = null;
          }
          if (boardSquares[dragStart]) {
            const img = boardSquares[dragStart].querySelector("img");
            if (img) img.style.opacity = "1";
          }
          const el = document.elementFromPoint(cx, cy);
          const t = el?.closest?.(".square");
          const to = t ? t.dataset.square : null;
          if (to && to !== dragStart) {
            makeMove(dragStart, to);
          } else if (to === dragStart) {
            selectedSquare = selectedSquare === dragStart ? null : dragStart;
            updateBoard();
          }
          cleanup();
        }
        function cleanup() {
          if (dragClone) {
            dragClone.remove();
            dragClone = null;
          }
          if (rafId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
            rafId = null;
          }
          dragStart = null;
          isDragging = false;
        }
        function onClick(e) {
          if (isDragging) return;
          if (game.turn() !== myColor || game.game_over()) return;
          const sq = e.target.closest?.(".square");
          if (!sq) return;
          const s = sq.dataset.square;
          if (selectedSquare === null) {
            const p = game.get(s);
            if (p && p.color === myColor) {
              selectedSquare = s;
              updateBoard();
            }
          } else {
            makeMove(selectedSquare, s);
            selectedSquare = null;
          }
        }

        // --- رویدادهای Socket.io ---
        socket.on("connect", () => {
          if (currentGameId) {
            // اگه داشتم بازی می‌کردم و رفرش کردم، درخواست برگشتم رو میدم
            socket.emit("reconnectGame", { gameId: currentGameId });
          } else {
            // اگه تازه اومدم، دنبال حریف می‌گردم
            socket.emit("findGame");
          }
        });

        socket.on("waiting", (data) => {
          myName = data.name;
          localStorage.setItem("chesshub_player_name", myName);
          document.getElementById("statusBar").className = "status-bar waiting";
          document.getElementById("statusBar").textContent =
            `در انتظار حریف... (شما: ${myName})`;
          document.getElementById("msgBox").textContent =
            "لطفاً صبر کنید تا یک نفر پیدا شود.";
        });

        socket.on("gameStart", (data) => {
          currentGameId = data.gameId;
          localStorage.setItem("chesshub_game_id", currentGameId);

          myColor = data.color;
          isFlipped = myColor === "b";
          game = new Chess(data.fen);
          buildBoard();

          document.getElementById("statusBar").className = "status-bar";
          let oppText = data.opponent ? ` | حریف: ${data.opponent}` : "";
          let reconnectText = data.reconnect ? " (ادامه بازی پس از قطعی)" : "";
          document.getElementById("statusBar").textContent =
            `شما ${myColor === "w" ? "سفید" : "سیاه"} هستید${reconnectText}${oppText}`;
          document.getElementById("msgBox").textContent =
            "بازی در حال انجام است.";

          // اضافه کردن رویدادهای موس و لمسی
          boardDiv.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
          window.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
          window.addEventListener("mouseup", onUp);
          boardDiv.addEventListener("touchstart", onDown, { passive: false });
          boardDiv.addEventListener("touchmove", onMove, { passive: false });
          boardDiv.addEventListener("touchend", onUp, { passive: false });
          boardDiv.addEventListener("click", onClick);
        });

        // سرور حرکت رو تایید کرد و وضعیت جدید رو فرستاد
        socket.on("moveMade", (data) => {
          game.load(data.fen);
          updateBoard();
        });

        // اگه بازی قبلی تموم شده بود یا پیدا نشد، دوباره بگرد
        socket.on("findNewGame", () => {
          localStorage.removeItem("chesshub_game_id");
          currentGameId = null;
          socket.emit("findGame");
        });

        socket.on("gameOver", (data) => {
          document.getElementById("statusBar").textContent =
            `بازی تمام شد! (${data.result})`;
          localStorage.removeItem("chesshub_game_id");
          localStorage.removeItem("chesshub_player_name");
        });

        socket.on("opponentLeft", () => {
          document.getElementById("statusBar").textContent =
            "حریف بازی رو ترک کرد! برنده شدی.";
          localStorage.removeItem("chesshub_game_id");
        });

        buildBoard(); // ساخت تخته خالی تا قبل از اتصال
      })();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

        cursor: pointer;
        touch-action: none;
        position: relative;
      }
      .square.light {
        background-color: var(--board-light, #f0d9b5);
      }
      .square.dark {
        background-color: var(--board-dark, #b58863);
      }
      .square.selected {
        background-color: #f6e05e !important;
      }
      .square.hint-from {
        background-color: rgba(49, 130, 206, 0.2) !important;
        box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 3px rgba(49, 130, 206, 0.5);
      }
      .square.hint-to {
        background-color: rgba(49, 130, 206, 0.4) !important;
        box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 3px rgba(49, 130, 206, 0.9);
      }
      .piece-img {
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        pointer-events: none;
        user-select: none;
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        z-index: 1;
      }

      .controls-center {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 10px;
        margin-top: 12px;
      }
      .btn {
        background: #f1f5f9;
        border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;

        border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
        font-weight: 700;
        cursor: pointer;
        transition: var(--transition-smooth);
        font-size: 0.85rem;
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 6px;
        font-family: "Vazir";
        color: var(--text-dark);
      }
      .btn-success {
        background: var(--success);
        color: white;
        border: 1px solid #2f855a;
        box-shadow: 0 4px 0 #2f855a;
      }
      .btn-success:hover {
        background: #48bb78;
        transform: translateY(-1px);
      }
      .btn-success:active {
        transform: translateY(3px);
        box-shadow: 0 1px 0 #2f855a;
      }
      .btn-danger {
        background: var(--danger);
        color: white;
        border: 1px solid #c53030;
        box-shadow: 0 4px 0 #c53030;
      }
      .btn-danger:hover {
        background: #fc8181;
        transform: translateY(-1px);
      }

      .message-box {
        margin-top: 12px;
        padding: 8px 14px;
        border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
        font-size: 0.85rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease;
        text-align: center;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 8px;

      @keyframes fadeIn {
        from {
          opacity: 0;
          transform: translateY(6px);
        }
        to {
          opacity: 1;
          transform: translateY(0);
        }
      }
      .info {
        background: #f8fafc;
        color: var(--text-light);
        border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;

        border: 1px solid #feb2b2;

        display: none;

      <div class="practice-area">

              ><i class="fas fa-tag"></i>

              ><i class="fas fa-chess-knight"></i>
              <span id="statTurnEnd">نوبت: -</span></span
            >
          </div>
          <div id="chessboard" style="display: none"></div>
          <!-- تخته مشترک است -->
          <div class="controls-center">

              <i class="fas fa-play"></i> موقعیت جدید

        <!-- تب ۳: تایم تریال -->
        <div class="tab-content" id="tab-timetrial">
          <div class="stats-bar">
            <span
              ><i class="far fa-clock"></i>

                >امتیاز: 0</span

              <i class="fas fa-bolt"></i> شروع تریال
            </button>
            <button
              class="btn btn-danger"
              id="stopTrialBtn"
              style="display: none"
            >
              <i class="fas fa-stop"></i> توقف
            </button>
          </div>
          <div class="message-box info" id="trialMsg">
            برای شروع دکمه را بزنید...
          </div>
        </div>

    <script src="js/script.js"></script>
    <script>
      (function () {
        "use strict";
        (function applySavedTheme() {
          try {
            const s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("chesshub_settings"));
            if (s && s.theme) document.body.classList.add(`theme-${s.theme}`);
          } catch (e) {}
        })();

        let game = new Chess();
        let boardSquares = {};
        let selectedSquare = null;
        let isFlipped = false;
        let pieceSet = "neo";
        const pieceCodes = {
          wk: "wk.png",
          wq: "wq.png",
          wr: "wr.png",
          wb: "wb.png",
          wn: "wn.png",
          wp: "wp.png",
          bk: "bk.png",
          bq: "bq.png",
          br: "br.png",
          bb: "bb.png",
          bn: "bn.png",
          bp: "bp.png",
        };
        function getCurrentPieceSet() {
          try {
            const s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("chesshub_settings"));
            return s?.pieceSet || "neo";
          } catch {
            return "neo";
          }
        }

        const boardDiv = document.getElementById("chessboard");

        // --- رندر تخته ---
        function buildBoard() {
          boardDiv.innerHTML = "";
          boardSquares = {};
          for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
              const row = isFlipped ? 7 - i : i,
                col = isFlipped ? 7 - j : j;
              const sqName = String.fromCharCode(97 + col) + (8 - row);
              const sq = document.createElement("div");
              sq.className = `square ${(row + col) % 2 === 0 ? "light" : "dark"}`;
              sq.dataset.square = sqName;
              const img = document.createElement("img");
              img.classList.add("piece-img");
              img.draggable = false;
              img.style.display = "none";
              sq.appendChild(img);
              boardDiv.appendChild(sq);
              boardSquares[sqName] = { div: sq, img: img };
            }
          }
          updateBoard();
        }

        function updateBoard() {
          const b = game.board();
          for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
              const row = isFlipped ? 7 - i : i,
                col = isFlipped ? 7 - j : j;
              const p = b[row][col];
              const sqName = String.fromCharCode(97 + col) + (8 - row);
              const sqData = boardSquares[sqName];
              if (!sqData) continue;
              sqData.div.classList.remove("selected");
              if (p) {
                const key =
                  (p.color === "w" ? "w" : "b") + p.type.toLowerCase();
                const src = `pieces/${pieceSet}/${pieceCodes[key]}`;
                if (sqData.img.src.indexOf(src) === -1) sqData.img.src = src;
                sqData.img.style.display = "block";
              } else {
                sqData.img.style.display = "none";
              }
            }
          }
          if (selectedSquare && boardSquares[selectedSquare])
            boardSquares[selectedSquare].div.classList.add("selected");
        }

        // --- دیتابیس پازل‌ها ---
        let allPuzzles = [];
        let currentPuzzle = null;
        let puzzleMoveIndex = 0;
        let activeTab = "weakness";

        async function loadPuzzleDatabase() {
          try {
            const ids = ["weaknessMsg", "endgameMsg"];
            ids.forEach((id) => {
              const el = document.getElementById(id);
              el.className = "message-box loading";
              el.innerHTML =
                '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> در حال بارگذاری دیتابیس...';
            });

            // استفاده از نام فایل صحیح: puzzles.txt
            const response = await fetch("data/puzzles.txt");
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error("File not found");
            const text = await response.text();
            const lines = text.split("\n");

            allPuzzles = [];
            for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
              const line = lines[i].trim();
              if (!line) continue;

              const parts = line.split(",");
              if (parts.length < 8) continue;

              const fen = parts[1];
              const movesStr = parts[2];
              const themes = parts[7];

              if (!fen || !movesStr) continue;

              const turn = fen.split(" ")[1];
              const botColor = turn;
              const userColor = botColor === "w" ? "b" : "w";

              allPuzzles.push({
                fen: fen,
                moves: movesStr.split(" "),
                themes: themes,
                userColor: userColor,
              });
            }

            ids.forEach((id) => {
              document.getElementById(id).className = "message-box success";
              document.getElementById(id).textContent = `دیتابیس آماده شد!`;
            });
            loadPuzzle("weakness");
          } catch (e) {
            console.error(e);
            const ids = ["weaknessMsg", "endgameMsg"];
            ids.forEach((id) => {
              document.getElementById(id).className = "message-box error";
              document.getElementById(id).textContent =
                "خطا در بارگذاری data/puzzles.txt";
            });
          }
        }

        // --- تب‌ها ---
        document.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
            if (isTimeTrial && btn.dataset.tab !== "timetrial") {
              alert("برای تغییر بخش، ابتدا تایم تریال را متوقف کنید!");
              return;
            }

            // مخفی کردن تخته در تب‌های غیرفعال و نمایش در تب فعال
            document
              .querySelectorAll("#chessboard")
              .forEach((b) => (b.style.display = "none"));
            const activeContent = document.getElementById(
              `tab-${btn.dataset.tab}`,
            );
            const activeBoard = activeContent.querySelector("#chessboard");
            if (activeBoard) activeBoard.style.display = "grid";

            // جابجایی المان تخته به تب فعال
            activeBoard.parentNode.insertBefore(boardDiv, activeBoard);
            activeBoard.remove(); // حذف تگ خالی جایگزین
            boardDiv.id = "chessboard"; // اطمینان از ID

            document
              .querySelectorAll(".tab-btn")
              .forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
            document
              .querySelectorAll(".tab-content")
              .forEach((c) => c.classList.remove("active"));
            btn.classList.add("active");
            activeTab = btn.dataset.tab;
            activeContent.classList.add("active");

            buildBoard(); // رندر مجدد تخته در موقعیت جدید
          });
        });

        document.querySelectorAll("#weaknessChips .chip").forEach((c) =>
          c.addEventListener("click", () => {
            document
              .querySelectorAll("#weaknessChips .chip")
              .forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
            c.classList.add("active");
            if (!isTimeTrial) loadPuzzle("weakness");
          }),
        );

        document.querySelectorAll("#endgameChips .chip").forEach((c) =>
          c.addEventListener("click", () => {
            document
              .querySelectorAll("#endgameChips .chip")
              .forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
            c.classList.add("active");
            loadPuzzle("endgame");
          }),
        );

        document
          .getElementById("loadWeaknessBtn")
          .addEventListener("click", () => loadPuzzle("weakness"));
        document
          .getElementById("loadEndgameBtn")
          .addEventListener("click", () => loadPuzzle("endgame"));

        function loadPuzzle(mode) {
          if (allPuzzles.length === 0) return;

          let theme = "all";
          let msgId = "weaknessMsg";
          let statTacticId = "statTactic";
          let statTurnId = "statTurn";

          if (mode === "weakness" || mode === "timetrial") {
            theme = document.querySelector("#weaknessChips .chip.active")
              .dataset.theme;
            msgId = isTimeTrial ? "trialMsg" : "weaknessMsg";
          } else if (mode === "endgame") {
            theme = document.querySelector("#endgameChips .chip.active").dataset
              .theme;
            msgId = "endgameMsg";
            statTacticId = "statEndgame";
            statTurnId = "statTurnEnd";
          }

          let pool = allPuzzles;
          if (theme !== "all") {
            // اصلاح فیلتر: تطبیق دقیق کلمه در لیست تم‌ها
            pool = allPuzzles.filter((p) => {
              if (!p.themes) return false;
              const themeArray = p.themes.trim().split(/\s+/);
              return themeArray.includes(theme);
            });
          }

          if (pool.length === 0) {
            document.getElementById(msgId).className = "message-box error";
            document.getElementById(msgId).textContent =
              "پازلی برای این دسته یافت نشد.";
            return;
          }

          currentPuzzle = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
          game = new Chess(currentPuzzle.fen);
          isFlipped = currentPuzzle.userColor === "b";
          puzzleMoveIndex = 0;
          selectedSquare = null;
          buildBoard();

          document.getElementById(statTacticId).textContent =
            `تاکتیک: ${currentPuzzle.themes.split(" ")[0] || "-"}`;

          const colorName = currentPuzzle.userColor === "w" ? "سفید" : "سیاه";
          document.getElementById(statTurnId).textContent =
            `نوبت: ${colorName}`;

          document.getElementById(msgId).className = "message-box info";
          document.getElementById(msgId).textContent =
            `بات حرکت اول را انجام داد. نوبت شماست (${colorName}).`;

          setTimeout(() => playBotMove(), 800);
        }

        function playBotMove() {
          if (!currentPuzzle || puzzleMoveIndex >= currentPuzzle.moves.length)
            return;

          const uci = currentPuzzle.moves[puzzleMoveIndex];
          const from = uci.slice(0, 2),
            to = uci.slice(2, 4);
          game.move({ from, to, promotion: uci[4] || "q" });
          puzzleMoveIndex++;

          if (boardSquares[from])
            boardSquares[from].div.classList.add("hint-from");
          if (boardSquares[to]) boardSquares[to].div.classList.add("hint-to");
          updateBoard();

          setTimeout(() => {
            if (boardSquares[from])
              boardSquares[from].div.classList.remove("hint-from");
            if (boardSquares[to])
              boardSquares[to].div.classList.remove("hint-to");
          }, 1500);
        }

        // --- مود ۳: تایم تریال ---
        let isTimeTrial = false,
          timeLeft = 180,
          score = 0,
          trialInterval = null;
        document
          .getElementById("startTrialBtn")
          .addEventListener("click", () => {
            if (allPuzzles.length === 0) {
              alert("ابتدا صبر کنید تا دیتابیس لود شود!");
              return;
            }

            isTimeTrial = true;
            timeLeft = 180;
            score = 0;
            document.getElementById("startTrialBtn").style.display = "none";
            document.getElementById("stopTrialBtn").style.display =
              "inline-flex";

            document
              .querySelectorAll("#weaknessChips .chip")
              .forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
            document
              .querySelector('#weaknessChips .chip[data-theme="all"]')
              .classList.add("active");

            loadPuzzle("timetrial");
            updateTimerUI();

            trialInterval = setInterval(() => {
              timeLeft--;
              updateTimerUI();
              if (timeLeft <= 0) stopTimeTrial(true);
            }, 1000);
          });

        function stopTimeTrial(isTimeUp = false) {
          isTimeTrial = false;
          clearInterval(trialInterval);
          document.getElementById("startTrialBtn").style.display =
            "inline-flex";
          document.getElementById("stopTrialBtn").style.display = "none";
          document.getElementById("trialMsg").className = "message-box success";
          document.getElementById("trialMsg").textContent = isTimeUp
            ? `زمان تمام شد! امتیاز شما: ${score}`
            : `بازی متوقف شد. امتیاز شما: ${score}`;
        }

        function updateTimerUI() {
          const m = Math.floor(timeLeft / 60)
            .toString()
            .padStart(2, "0");
          const s = (timeLeft % 60).toString().padStart(2, "0");
          document.getElementById("timerDisplay").textContent = `${m}:${s}`;
          document.getElementById("scoreDisplay").textContent =
            `امتیاز: ${score}`;
        }

        document
          .getElementById("stopTrialBtn")
          .addEventListener("click", () => stopTimeTrial(false));

        // --- منطق حرکت و درگ‌اند‌دراپ ---
        let dragStart = null,
          isDragging = false,
          dragClone = null,
          rafId = null,
          lastX = 0,
          lastY = 0,
          cloneSize = 0;
        function createDragClone(sq, cx, cy) {
          const d = boardSquares[sq];
          if (!d || d.img.style.display == "none") return null;
          const r = d.div.getBoundingClientRect();
          cloneSize = r.width;
          const c = d.img.cloneNode(true);
          c.style.cssText = `position:fixed;pointer-events:none;z-index:9999;width:${cloneSize}px;height:${cloneSize}px;left:0;top:0;transform:translate3d(${cx - cloneSize / 2}px,${cy - cloneSize / 2}px,0) scale(1.05);`;
          document.body.appendChild(c);
          d.img.style.opacity = "0";
          return c;
        }
        function updDrag() {
          if (dragClone)
            dragClone.style.transform = `translate3d(${lastX - cloneSize / 2}px,${lastY - cloneSize / 2}px,0) scale(1.05)`;
          rafId = null;
        }
        function onDown(e) {
          const cx = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX,
            cy = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
          const el = document.elementFromPoint(cx, cy);
          const sq = el?.closest?.(".square");
          if (!sq) return;
          const s = sq.dataset.square;
          const p = game.get(s);
          if (p) {
            e.preventDefault();
            dragStart = s;
            isDragging = true;
            dragClone = createDragClone(s, cx, cy);
          } else if (selectedSquare) {
            makeMove(selectedSquare, s);
            selectedSquare = null;
            updateBoard();
          }
        }
        function onMove(e) {
          if (!isDragging) return;
          e.preventDefault();
          lastX = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
          lastY = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
          if (!rafId) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(updDrag);
        }
        function onUp(e) {
          if (!isDragging || !dragStart) {
            cleanup();
            return;
          }
          const cx = e.changedTouches ? e.changedTouches[0].clientX : e.clientX,
            cy = e.changedTouches ? e.changedTouches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
          if (dragClone) {
            dragClone.remove();
            dragClone = null;
          }
          if (boardSquares[dragStart])
            boardSquares[dragStart].img.style.opacity = "1";
          const el = document.elementFromPoint(cx, cy);
          const t = el?.closest?.(".square");
          const to = t ? t.dataset.square : null;
          if (to && to !== dragStart) {
            makeMove(dragStart, to);
          } else if (to === dragStart) {
            selectedSquare = selectedSquare === dragStart ? null : dragStart;
            updateBoard();
          }
          cleanup();
        }
        function cleanup() {
          if (dragClone) {
            dragClone.remove();
            dragClone = null;
          }
          if (rafId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
            rafId = null;
          }
          dragStart = null;
          isDragging = false;
        }
        function onClick(e) {
          if (isDragging) return;
          const sq = e.target.closest?.(".square");
          if (!sq) return;
          const s = sq.dataset.square;
          if (selectedSquare === null) {
            const p = game.get(s);
            if (p) {
              selectedSquare = s;
              updateBoard();
            }
          } else {
            makeMove(selectedSquare, s);
            selectedSquare = null;
            updateBoard();
          }
        }

        async function makeMove(from, to, promo = "q") {
          if (!currentPuzzle || puzzleMoveIndex >= currentPuzzle.moves.length)
            return;

          const move = game.move({ from, to, promotion: promo });
          if (!move) return;

          const playedUci = move.from + move.to + (move.promotion || "");
          const expectedUci = currentPuzzle.moves[puzzleMoveIndex];
          const msgId = isTimeTrial
            ? "trialMsg"
            : activeTab === "endgame"
              ? "endgameMsg"
              : "weaknessMsg";

          if (playedUci === expectedUci) {
            puzzleMoveIndex++;
            selectedSquare = null;
            updateBoard();

            if (puzzleMoveIndex < currentPuzzle.moves.length) {
              if (isTimeTrial) {
                document.getElementById("trialMsg").className =
                  "message-box info";
                document.getElementById("trialMsg").textContent =
                  "درست! بات در حال فکر کردن...";
              }
              setTimeout(() => playBotMove(), 600);
            } else {
              // پازل حل شد!
              if (isTimeTrial) {
                timeLeft += 5;
                score += 10;
                updateTimerUI();
                document.getElementById("trialMsg").className =
                  "message-box success";
                document.getElementById("trialMsg").textContent =
                  "آفرین! +10 امتیاز و +5 ثانیه. پازل بعدی...";
                setTimeout(() => loadPuzzle("timetrial"), 1500); // رفتن خودکار به پازل بعدی
              } else {
                document.getElementById(msgId).className =
                  "message-box success";
                document.getElementById(msgId).textContent =
                  "آفرین! درست حل شد. پازل بعدی...";
                setTimeout(() => loadPuzzle(activeTab), 1500); // رفتن خودکار به پازل بعدی
              }
            }
          } else {
            game.undo();
            updateBoard();
            if (isTimeTrial) {
              timeLeft -= 3;
              score = Math.max(0, score - 5);
              updateTimerUI();
              document.getElementById("trialMsg").className =
                "message-box error";
              document.getElementById("trialMsg").textContent =
                "اشتباه! -5 امتیاز و -3 ثانیه";
            } else {
              document.getElementById(msgId).className = "message-box error";
              document.getElementById(msgId).textContent =
                "اشتباه! دوباره فکر کن.";
            }
          }
        }

        // --- مقداردهی اولیه ---
        async function init() {
          pieceSet = getCurrentPieceSet();
          document.addEventListener("pieceSetChanged", () => {
            pieceSet = getCurrentPieceSet();
            updateBoard();
          });
          buildBoard();

          await loadPuzzleDatabase();

          boardDiv.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
          window.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
          window.addEventListener("mouseup", onUp);
          boardDiv.addEventListener("touchstart", onDown, { passive: false });
          boardDiv.addEventListener("touchmove", onMove, { passive: false });
          boardDiv.addEventListener("touchend", onUp, { passive: false });
          boardDiv.addEventListener("click", onClick);
        }
        if (document.readyState === "loading")
          document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", init);
        else init();
      })();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

        function loadSettings() {
          try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem("chesshub_settings");
            if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved);
          } catch (e) {}
          return { pieceSet: "neo", theme: "1" };
        }

        function saveSettings(settings) {
          try {
            localStorage.setItem("chesshub_settings", JSON.stringify(settings));
          } catch (e) {}
        }

        function buildThemeButtons() {
          const container = document.getElementById("themeGrid");
          if (!container) return;
          const currentTheme = loadSettings().theme || "1";
          container.innerHTML = "";
          themes.forEach((theme) => {
            const btn = document.createElement("button");
            btn.className = `theme-btn${theme.id === currentTheme ? " active" : ""}`;
            btn.dataset.theme = theme.id;
            btn.innerHTML = `
              <span class="theme-preview">
                <span class="preview-light" style="background:${theme.light};"></span>
                <span class="preview-dark" style="background:${theme.dark};"></span>
              </span>
            `;
            btn.onclick = () => {
              document
                .querySelectorAll(".theme-btn")
                .forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
              btn.classList.add("active");
              window._tempTheme = theme.id;
              showToast(`✅ تم انتخاب شد (برای اعمال ذخیره کن)`);
            };
            container.appendChild(btn);
          });
        }

        function buildPieceButtons() {
          const container = document.getElementById("pieceSetsContainer");
          if (!container) return;
          const currentPieceSet = loadSettings().pieceSet || "neo";
          container.innerHTML = "";
          let totalCount = 0;

          Object.keys(pieceSets).forEach((catName) => {
            const section = document.createElement("div");
            section.className = "piece-section";
            const label = document.createElement("span");
            label.className = "section-label";
            label.textContent = catName;
            section.appendChild(label);

            const grid = document.createElement("div");
            grid.className = "section-grid";

            pieceSets[catName].forEach((setName) => {
              totalCount++;
              const btn = document.createElement("button");
              btn.className = `piece-btn${setName === currentPieceSet ? " active" : ""}`;
              btn.dataset.set = setName;
              const wKPath = `pieces/${setName}/wk.png`;
              const bKPath = `pieces/${setName}/bk.png`;

              btn.innerHTML = `
                <div class="piece-preview">
                  <img src="${wKPath}" alt="شاه سفید" onerror="this.style.display='none';this.parentElement.querySelector('.piece-fallback-w').style.display='flex'">
                  <div class="piece-fallback piece-fallback-w" style="display:none;">♔</div>
                  <img src="${bKPath}" alt="شاه سیاه" onerror="this.style.display='none';this.parentElement.querySelector('.piece-fallback-b').style.display='flex'">
                  <div class="piece-fallback piece-fallback-b" style="display:none;">♚</div>
                </div>
              `;
              btn.onclick = () => {
                document
                  .querySelectorAll(".piece-btn")
                  .forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
                btn.classList.add("active");
                window._tempPieceSet = setName;
                showToast(`✅ مجموعه مهره انتخاب شد (برای اعمال ذخیره کن)`);
              };
              grid.appendChild(btn);
            });
            section.appendChild(grid);
            container.appendChild(section);
          });
          const countDisplay = document.getElementById("pieceCountDisplay");
          if (countDisplay) countDisplay.textContent = totalCount;
        }

        function saveAllSettings() {
          const settings = loadSettings();
          const newTheme = window._tempTheme || settings.theme || "1";
          const newPieceSet =
            window._tempPieceSet || settings.pieceSet || "neo";
          settings.theme = newTheme;
          settings.pieceSet = newPieceSet;
          saveSettings(settings);

          document.body.className = document.body.className
            .split(" ")
            .filter((cls) => !cls.startsWith("theme-"))
            .join(" ");
          document.body.classList.add(`theme-${newTheme}`);

          document.dispatchEvent(
            new CustomEvent("pieceSetChanged", {
              detail: { pieceSet: newPieceSet },
            }),
          );
          document.dispatchEvent(
            new CustomEvent("themeChanged", { detail: { theme: newTheme } }),
          );
          showToast(`✅ تنظیمات با موفقیت ذخیره شد`);
        }

        function resetToDefault() {
          if (!confirm("آیا مطمئنی؟ تنظیمات به حالت پیش‌فرض برمی‌گردد."))
            return;
          const settings = { pieceSet: "neo", theme: "1" };
          saveSettings(settings);
          document.body.className = document.body.className
            .split(" ")
            .filter((cls) => !cls.startsWith("theme-"))
            .join(" ");
          document.body.classList.add("theme-1");
          window._tempTheme = "1";
          window._tempPieceSet = "neo";
          buildThemeButtons();
          buildPieceButtons();
          document.dispatchEvent(
            new CustomEvent("pieceSetChanged", { detail: { pieceSet: "neo" } }),
          );
          document.dispatchEvent(
            new CustomEvent("themeChanged", { detail: { theme: "1" } }),
          );
          showToast("✅ تنظیمات به حالت پیش‌فرض بازگشت");
        }

        function showToast(text) {
          const toast = document.getElementById("settingsToast");
          if (!toast) return;
          toast.textContent = text;
          toast.classList.add("show");
          clearTimeout(toast._timeout);
          toast._timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            toast.classList.remove("show");
          }, 2500);
        }

        function init() {
          const settings = loadSettings();
          window._tempTheme = settings.theme || "1";
          window._tempPieceSet = settings.pieceSet || "neo";
          buildThemeButtons();
          buildPieceButtons();
          document
            .getElementById("saveSettingsBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", saveAllSettings);
          document
            .getElementById("resetSettingsBtn")
            .addEventListener("click", resetToDefault);
        }

        if (document.readyState === "loading") {
          document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", init);
        } else {
          init();
        }
      })();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

FILE: C:\Users\VICTUS\chesshub\server.js
========================================================================================================================
const express = require("express");
const http = require("http");
const path = require("path");
const { Server } = require("socket.io");
const { Chess } = require("chess.js");
const app = express();
app.use(express.static(__dirname));
const server = http.createServer(app);
const io = new Server(server);

app.get("/", (req, res) => {
  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "online.html"));
});

let waitingPlayer = null;
const games = {};

// ساخت اسم تصادفی برای بازیکن
function generatePlayerName() {
  return "Player_" + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8);
}

io.on("connection", (socket) => {
  // به هر کاربر یه اسم اختصاص میدیم
  socket.playerName = generatePlayerName();
  console.log(`${socket.playerName} وصل شد`);

  // درخواست شروع بازی
  socket.on("findGame", () => {
    if (waitingPlayer) {
      const gameId = `game_${Date.now()}`;
      const chess = new Chess();

      games[gameId] = {
        chess: chess,
        players: { w: waitingPlayer, b: socket },
        fen: chess.fen(),
      };

      waitingPlayer.join(gameId);
      socket.join(gameId);

      // به هر دو نفر پیام شروع رو با اسم حریفشون می‌فرستیم
      waitingPlayer.emit("gameStart", {
        gameId,
        color: "w",
        fen: games[gameId].fen,
        opponent: socket.playerName,
      });
      socket.emit("gameStart", {
        gameId,
        color: "b",
        fen: games[gameId].fen,
        opponent: waitingPlayer.playerName,
      });

      waitingPlayer = null;
    } else {
      // اگه کسی تو انتظار نبود، این کاربر میذاریمش تو لیست انتظار
      waitingPlayer = socket;
      socket.emit("waiting", { name: socket.playerName });
    }
  });

  // برگشت به بازی بعد از رفرش (اصلاح شده)
  socket.on("reconnectGame", ({ gameId }) => {
    const game = games[gameId];
    if (game && (game.players.w === socket || game.players.b === socket)) {
      // بازیکن به بازی برمی‌گرده
      socket.join(gameId);
      const color = game.players.w === socket ? "w" : "b";
      socket.emit("gameStart", {
        gameId,
        color,
        fen: game.fen,
        reconnect: true,
      });
    } else {
      // بازی پیدا نشد یا تمام شده، باید بازی جدید بگرده
      socket.emit("findNewGame");
    }
  });

  // انجام حرکت (اعتبارسنجی روی سرور)
  socket.on("makeMove", ({ gameId, from, to, promotion }) => {
    const game = games[gameId];
    if (!game) return;

    const playerColor = game.players.w === socket ? "w" : "b";
    if (game.chess.turn() !== playerColor) return; // تقلب نکن!

    try {
      const move = game.chess.move({ from, to, promotion: promotion || "q" });
      if (move) {
        game.fen = game.chess.fen();
        // حرکت رو برای هر دو نفر می‌فرستیم
        io.to(gameId).emit("moveMade", { fen: game.fen });

        if (game.chess.game_over()) {
          io.to(gameId).emit("gameOver", {
            result: game.chess.in_checkmate() ? "checkmate" : "draw",
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // حرکت غیرمجاز بود
    }
  });

  // قطع ارتباط
  socket.on("disconnect", () => {
    if (waitingPlayer === socket) {
      waitingPlayer = null;
    }
    // اگه تو بازی بود، به حریفش خبر می‌دیم
    for (const gameId in games) {
      if (
        games[gameId].players.w === socket ||
        games[gameId].players.b === socket
      ) {
        io.to(gameId).emit("opponentLeft");
        setTimeout(() => delete games[gameId], 300000);
      }
    }
  });
});

server.listen(3000, () => {
  console.log("سرور ارتقا‌یافته آماده است! http://localhost:3000");
});
